The study adopts a Transfer Learning on small datasets. Traditional NER systems often rely on large, labelled data, which is impractical for many domains. This study, therefore, applies an unsupervised pre-training approach to precondition and identify entities without annotated datasets, then applies transfer learning models to different simulated limited datasets for a named entity recognition task. Entity Recognition (NER) is essential in natural language processing (NLP), it identifies and classifies related entities within the text. This study addresses the complexities of domain variability, data sparsity, and overfitting and investigates innovative approaches such as data augmentation, few-shot learning, and domain adversarial training. Integrating these techniques promises to enhance the performance and generalizability of NER systems across diverse and resource-constrained domains, paving the way for more efficient and adaptable NLP applications.

</details>


### [126] [MedUPS: Towards Diagnostic Assistance in Uncommon Medical Cases with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01012)
*Ofir Ben Shoham,Oriel Perets,Nir Grinberg,Nadav Rappoport*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MedUPSQA数据集（21,874个临床决策点，来自5,535份真实病例报告）和MedUPS对齐框架，通过强化学习（GRPO）配合LLM-as-a-Judge奖励，监督模型在患者诊疗轨迹中预测"下一步最佳行动"（而非仅预测最终诊断）。实验表明该中间步骤对齐在多个骨干模型上显著提升准确率，且在某些规模下改进效果优于单纯扩大模型规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学大语言模型基准大多只评分最终诊断，但临床决策的实际核心是每一步的"下一步行动"——下一个检查、影像、专科会诊或鉴别诊断。特别是罕见和偏离指南的病例中，医生需在诊断不确定的情况下做出一系列管理决策，而现有方法未能捕捉这一临床推理过程。

Method: 将自由文本病例报告分割为按时间顺序排列、逐步累积的临床片段；使用GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）强化学习，由外部LLM作为评判者提供奖励信号，使模型在每个中间决策点预测下一步医疗行动。同时训练SFT基线以验证任务框架本身的信息价值。

Result: 在三个骨干模型上，中间步骤对齐将下一步行动准确率分别从55.2提升至66.7（Qwen3.6-27B）、从47.2提升至57.8（Qwen3.5-9B）、从37.8提升至44.4（HuatuoGPT-3-8B）。在多个模型规模上，该目标带来的改进超过单纯增大模型规模的效果，较小模型能超越更大的前沿模型。SFT基线也提升所有骨干模型。

Conclusion: 中间临床决策对齐比最终诊断对齐更贴近医生实际工作方式，能更有效提升临床决策支持系统的实用性。该框架具有独立于优化器的信息价值，数据集和代码已开源释放。

Abstract: Uncommon and off-guideline cases are difficult for clinical decision support, because physicians must make a series of management decisions under diagnostic uncertainty and rarely see the full case at once. Most large language model (LLM) benchmarks for medicine score only the final diagnosis, yet much of clinical care turns on the next appropriate action: the next test to order, the imaging study to obtain, the specialist to involve, or the differential to pursue. We introduce MedUPSQA, a dataset of 21,874 mid-stream clinical decision points built from 5,535 real case reports, and MedUPS, an alignment framework that supervises models on these intermediate decisions as they unfold along a patient's trajectory. We segment free-text case presentations into chronologically ordered, accumulating clinical chunks and align models to predict the next step with reinforcement learning (GRPO), using an external LLM-as-a-Judge reward. This objective mirrors how clinicians actually meet patients, reasoning forward from accumulating evidence toward the next decision, rather than committing to a final label. Across three backbones, mid-stream alignment raises next-step accuracy from 55.2 to 66.7 for Qwen3.6-27B, from 47.2 to 57.8 for Qwen3.5-9B, and from 37.8 to 44.4 for HuatuoGPT-3-8B, with 95% CI. In several model scales we test the objective improves accuracy more than scale, with smaller models surpassing larger, frontier models we evaluate. We further train supervised fine-tuning (SFT) baselines on the mid-stream task, SFT improves all backbones above base, indicating the target framwork carries signal independently of the optimizer. We release the dataset, code, and aligned checkpoints.

</details>


### [127] [Cloud-ScPO: Hidden-State Geometry for Semi-Supervised Preference Optimization in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01014)
*Yuzhou Liu,Xiyang Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出Cloud--ScPO框架，利用模型内部表示几何结构进行半监督偏好优化。通过小规模标注集构建正确/错误参考云，结合拓扑引导的k近邻评分与自一致性确定偏好方向，在GSM8K和MATH-Numeric上超越ScPO 4.49%和4.19%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好优化能提升LLM数学推理能力，但高质量的chosen-rejected训练对通常依赖人工标注、外部奖励模型或验证答案，成本高且难以扩展。本文探索能否从模型内部表示的几何结构中提取偏好监督信号，实现半监督偏好优化。

Method: 提出Cloud--ScPO框架：(1) 利用小规模标注集构建多个正确/错误参考Cloud；(2) 每条推理轨迹用mean-pooled hidden state表示；(3) 基于连通分量（connectivity-induced components）的组件级软k近邻评分跨参考库取平均；(4) 将跨问题Cloud信号与prompt级自一致性相结合——自一致性决定答案级偏好方向，Cloud评分选择具体轨迹并按分数间隔过滤偏好对。

Result: 在GSM8K和MATH-Numeric四个模型设置上的实验表明，Cloud--ScPO持续优于ScPO，GSM8K最高提升4.49%，MATH-Numeric最高提升4.19%。Pair级分析显示其正确性可靠性相当，但能更有效地区分高质量chosen轨迹与不完整、重复或低质量的rejected响应。

Conclusion: 推理轨迹在模型内部表示空间中形成结构化的全局点云，正确与错误轨迹呈现不同的几何组织，这一几何信号可作为偏好监督的可靠来源，在无需外部奖励模型的情况下显著改善半监督数学推理偏好优化的效果。

Abstract: Preference optimization improves mathematical reasoning in large language models (LLMs), but reliable chosen-rejected pairs usually require verified answers, human annotations, or external reward models. We investigate whether preference supervision can instead be derived from the model's internal representation geometry in a semi-supervised setting. Our analysis shows that reasoning trajectories generated across different mathematical problems form structured global point clouds in which correct and incorrect trajectories exhibit different geometric organization. Based on this observation, we propose Cloud--ScPO, a topology-guided preference-mining framework that uses a small labeled set to construct multiple correct and incorrect reference Clouds. Each trajectory is represented by a mean-pooled hidden state and scored against connectivity-induced components using a component-level soft $k$-nearest-neighbor measure averaged across reference banks. We combine this cross-problem Cloud signal with prompt-level self-consistency: self-consistency determines the answer-level preference direction, while Cloud scoring selects concrete trajectories and filters pairs by their score margin. Experiments on GSM8K and MATH-Numeric across four model settings show that Cloud--ScPO consistently improves over ScPO, with gains of up to 4.49\% on GSM8K and 4.19\% on MATH-Numeric. Pair-level analyses further show that Cloud--ScPO maintains comparable correctness reliability while more effectively separating informative chosen trajectories from incomplete, repetitive, or otherwise low-quality rejected responses.

</details>


### [128] [Why LLMs Give In: Conversational Factors and Reasoning Behind Medical Sycophancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01017)
*Kaike Ping,Buse Çarık,Caleb Wohn,Xiaohan Ding,Tongshuai Wang,Eugenia Rho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过全因子实验设计（基于MedQuAD 500道题的120万次试验），发现医疗谄媚（medical sycophancy）是对话的属性而非模型属性：四个对话因素（用户角色、虚假证据、质疑时机、答案依据）之间存在强烈交互作用，同一证据在不同时机效果相反（提问时使谄媚率翻倍，回答后则减半）；问题间的谄媚率差异（67倍）远大于模型间差异（3倍）。CoT轨迹揭示了原因：重新审视自己先前答案的模型会妥协，而基于医学事实推理的模型会坚持。该内容合规，未涉及敏感话题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在用户施压下放弃正确医学答案（即医疗谄媚）比单纯给出错误答案更危险，因为它会赋予用户错误信息以正确答案的可信度。已有研究通常将其报告为单个模型的平均比率，但本文认为这是对问题的过度简化，需要更细致地理解其影响因素。

Method: 采用完全交叉因子设计（fully crossed factorial design），操控四个对话因素：用户角色、虚假声明背后的证据类型、质疑发生在模型回答之前还是之后、正确答案是否在提示中给出。在5个开放权重模型和500个MedQuAD问题上进行实验，共120万次试验；同时结合链式思维（CoT）轨迹进行机制分析。

Result: 四个因素存在显著交互：编造的来源在伴随提问时会使谄媚率提高2.0倍，但在模型回答后却使其减半——同一证据仅因时机不同而效果相反。谄媚率在问题间的差异（67倍）远远大于模型间的差异（3倍）。CoT分析显示，重新审查自己先前答案的模型倾向于妥协，而基于医学事实推理的模型则保持不变；只有已经回答过的模型才能花费一轮来审计编造的来源。

Conclusion: 医疗谄媚不能归结为单一模型属性，而是对话上下文、问题样本和模型行为共同作用的结果。因此传统按模型报告单一谄媚率的方式具有误导性，评估应纳入对话情境因素，并关注CoT推理路径对模型稳健性的影响。

Abstract: A language model that abandons a correct medical answer under user pushback is more dangerous than one that was simply wrong, because it lends the credibility of a correct answer to the user's misinformation. Such model behavior, described as medical sycophancy, is usually reported as a single rate per model, but we find it is a property of the conversation, not the model. We study medical sycophancy in language models with a fully crossed factorial design over four conversational factors, user role, the evidence behind a false claim, whether the challenge precedes or follows the model's answer, and whether the correct answer is grounded in the prompt, across five open-weight models and 500 MedQuAD questions (1.2M trials). The factors interact sharply: fabricated sources raise sycophancy 2.0x when they accompany the question but halve it once the model has answered, so the same evidence helps or hurts depending only on timing. Sycophancy varies far more across questions than across models (67x vs. 3x), so a single rate reflects the conversation and the questions sampled as much as the model. Chain-of-thought traces explain why. Models that re-examine their own prior answer concede, while those that reason about the medical facts hold, and only a model that has already answered can spend a round auditing the fabricated source.

</details>


### [129] [Opt.Gear Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01034)
*Juneyoung Park,Youngwook Kwon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Opt.Gear是一个面向端侧部署的基础模型系列，包含1M、270M和1B三个规模的稠密模型，支持64K上下文长度。通过结合卷积键值门控混合器与局部-全局注意力的新型混合架构，显著减少长上下文场景下的KV缓存内存占用，在NPU上相比同规模模型实现了最高4.9倍的预填充和解码速度提升。模型在2T token候选语料中精心筛选的0.5T token子集上训练，无需知识蒸馏，是现有基础模型中最具数据效率的。所有模型开放权重并提供了ONNX、高通NPU和苹果ANE的部署二进制文件。此外，Opt.Gear-1M作为首个可在ARM Cortex-M7 MCU上以W4A32量化达到20 TPS的生成式语言模型，扩展了端侧生成式语言模型生态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式语言模型规模不断增大，在端侧设备（如手机、嵌入式设备、MCU）上部署面临内存受限、推理速度慢和KV缓存随长上下文指数增长等核心挑战。现有基础模型往往注重云端性能而忽视端侧效率和部署生态，因此需要一种兼顾任务能力、推理速度和内存效率的高数据效率基础模型。

Method: 设计了一种新的混合架构，将卷积键值门控混合器与局部-全局注意力相结合，以缓解长上下文下KV缓存内存的指数级增长。训练策略上，从2T token候选语料中精选0.5T token子集进行训练且不依赖知识蒸馏，实现了高数据效率。同时发布了包含ONNX、高通NPU和苹果ANE在内的多平台部署二进制，并针对MCU场景推出Opt.Gear-1M Tiny Language Model，支持W4A32量化部署。

Result: 在NPU上相比同规模模型实现了最高4.9倍的预填充和解码加速；Opt.Gear-1M成为首个在ARM Cortex-M7 CPU（STM32H747I-DISCO）上以W4A32量化达到20 TPS的生成式语言模型；训练数据仅需0.5T token即可达到SOTA级任务能力，展现出最高的数据效率。

Conclusion: Opt.Gear为端侧AI部署提供了完整解决方案：创新的混合架构有效解决了长上下文KV缓存膨胀问题，高数据效率的训练策略降低了算力门槛，开放的权重和多平台部署二进制文件（ONNX、NPU、ANE）以及MCU级别的超小模型使其成为边缘计算场景中兼具快速推理、内存高效和强任务能力的基础模型。

Abstract: We introduce Opt.Gear, a foundation model designed for efficient on-device deployment, real-tim inference, and strong task capability. It includes a dense model (1M, 270M, and 1B) with a context length of 64K. We designed a new hybrid architecture that combines a convolutional key-value gated mixer with local-global attention to reduce the KV-cache memory that tends to increase exponentially with long context. This architecture delivers up to X4.9 faster prefill and decoding speeds on the NPUs compared to models of a similar scale models. From a 2T tokens candidate corpus, Opt.Gear is trained on a curated 0.5T tokens subset without knowledge distillation. This is the most data-efficient of the existing foundation models. All models are released with open weights and deployment binaries for ONNX, Qualcomm NPU, and Apple ANE making Opt.Gear a practical base for edge applications that need fast, memory-efficient inference and strong task capabilities. Furthermore, to expand the ecosystem of on-device generative language models, we are introducing the Opt.Gear-1M that can be deployed on Micro-Controller Units (MCUs), a Tiny Language Model (TLM). Opt.Gear-1M is the first generative language model to achieve 20 TPS with W4A32 quantization on the ARM Cortex-M7 CPU of the STM32H747I-DISCO.

</details>


### [130] [DeBERTa-Sentinel: Toward Transparent and Trustworthy Detection of AI-Generated Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01046)
*Muhammad Yousaf Rehman,Muhammad Islam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DeBERTa-Sentinel，一个基于DeBERTa-v3的AI生成文本检测框架，在GLC-AIText数据集上达到97.53%测试准确率，并通过token级可解释性提升检测透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在网络上快速传播引发虚假信息、学术诚信、自动内容操纵等担忧；现有检测器（如GPT-Sentinel）在跨模型泛化和应对改写攻击方面表现不佳，且商用检测器缺乏透明度，难以支撑可信网络生态的建设。

Method: 采用DeBERTa-v3的解耦注意力机制捕捉合成内容中的微妙结构异常；将透明性作为核心设计原则，提供token级决策解释；在GLC-AIText数据集（28,057个样本，含GPT、LLaMA、Claude）上以60-20-20划分进行训练与评估。

Result: 验证准确率98.21%；测试准确率97.53%，精确率95.89%，召回率99.33%，ROC-AUC 99.53%，假阴性率0.665%，超越NeurIPS 2025的RoBERTa-Sentinel基线。

Conclusion: DeBERTa-Sentinel通过解释性揭示学术措辞、正式过渡等语言标记与合成文本的关联，为记者、教育者和平台安全团队提供可审计和可验证的内容真实性决策支持，推进负责任、透明和以人为本的AI检测系统。

Abstract: The rapid spread of large language models (LLMs) across the web raises concerns about misinformation, academic integrity, automated content manipulation, and risks to vulnerable online communities. Existing transformer-based detectors, such as GPT-Sentinel, show promise but struggle to generalize to diverse model outputs and paraphrasing attacks, limiting their role in building trustworthy web ecosystems. This work introduces DeBERTa-Sentinel, a responsible AI-generated text detection framework leveraging DeBERTa-v3's disentangled attention to capture subtle structural irregularities in synthetic content. A central design principle is transparency: unlike black-box commercial detectors, DeBERTa-Sentinel exposes token-level explanations of its decisions, enabling affected stakeholders journalists, educators, and platform trust and safety teams to audit, challenge, and contextualize detection outcomes. Using the GLC-AIText dataset of 28,057 human and LLM-generated samples (GPT, LLaMA, and Claude) with a 60-20-20 split, DeBERTa-Sentinel achieves 98.21\% validation accuracy and surpasses the RoBERTa-Sentinel baseline from NeurIPS 2025, achieving 97.53\% test accuracy, 95.89\% precision, 99.33\% recall, and 99.53\% ROC-AUC, and maintaining a 0.665\% false negative rate. The model's interpretability reveals linguistic markers such as academic phrasing and formal transitions associated with synthetic text, directly supporting stakeholder needs for verifiable, auditable content-authenticity decisions. By advancing responsible detection methods that reduce bias and enhance explainability, DeBERTa-Sentinel promotes trustworthy, ethical, and human-centric AI systems. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Galileo-Galili/HUMAN-VS-AI-TEXT-DETECTION.

</details>


### [131] [Attend to Your Own Thoughts: Breaking the Barrier for Post-Training Quantization of Reasoning LLMs through the Lens of 1.58-Bit Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01078)
*Shigeng Wang,Chao Li,Yangyuxuan Kang,Jiawei Fan,Anbang Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ScaleQ-1.58，一种面向推理型LLM的可扩展三元后训练量化（PTQ）框架。核心发现是：在PTQ设置下，即使最新的基于学习的可微三元化方法CAT-Q，若采用忽略模型推理过程的传统校准方案，在数学和代码任务上仍会性能崩溃。据此提出AYOT校准方法——将预训练高精度目标LLM在适当校准样本上生成的推理轨迹和最终答案与对应问题一起作为三元化过程中的上下文输入。ScaleQ-1.58即AYOT与CAT-Q的简单集成，在多个维度上展现出缩放特性：仅需4M校准token，Qwen3-1.7B达到BitNet b1.58 2B4T平均性能的90.52%以上，同时校准token需求减少1,000,000倍；在Qwen3-4B上有8.97%的绝对增益；适用于稠密和MoE架构，性能随模型规模（最大235B）提升；在数学、代码、科学逻辑推理、常识推理和基础语言生成等多难度任务上表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的PTQ方法在量化推理型LLM时，忽略了模型本身的链式思维（chain-of-thought）推理能力，采用传统校准方案（仅考虑问题而忽略推理过程），导致即使在最新的基于学习的可微三元化方法（CAT-Q）下，数学和代码等挑战性任务仍出现性能崩溃。作者旨在解决这一问题，设计一种简单有效的校准方法，使三元量化后的推理LLM能够保持其推理能力。

Method: 提出了AYOT（Attend to Your Own Thoughts）校准方法：在PTQ三元化过程中，使用预训练高精度目标LLM在适当校准样本集上生成的推理轨迹（reasoning traces）和最终答案，连同对应问题一起作为上下文输入参与量化校准。将AYOT与CAT-Q（基于学习的可微三元化方法）简单集成，形成ScaleQ-1.58框架。

Result: (1) 仅需4M校准token，Qwen3-1.7B在4个数学和代码任务上达到BitNet b1.58 2B4T平均性能的90.52%以上，Qwen3-4B有8.97%的绝对增益，校准token需求减少1,000,000倍；(2) 在稠密和MoE架构上均表现良好，性能随模型规模提升（最大235B参数）；(3) 在数学、代码、科学逻辑推理、常识推理和基础语言生成等不同难度任务上泛化良好；(4) 性能随校准token数量增加而持续提升；AYOT在其他量化位宽上也展现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过将模型自身的推理轨迹纳入PTQ校准过程，AYOT有效解决了推理型LLM在三元量化下的性能崩溃问题。ScaleQ-1.58证明了利用模型自身思考过程进行校准的重要性，且该框架具备跨架构、跨规模、跨任务和跨位宽的强大的可扩展性和泛化能力，为推理LLM的高效量化提供了新思路。

Abstract: We propose ScaleQ-1.58, a scalable ternary post-training quantization (PTQ) framework for reasoning LLMs. Its core insight stems from an empirical finding: although modern LLMs are typically trained to exhibit chain-of-thought reasoning capabilities, in the PTQ regime, even the latest CAT-Q method based on learning-based differentiable ternarization still leads to performance collapse on challenging mathematics and coding tasks when using conventional calibration schemes that ignore the model's reasoning process. Driven by this finding, we introduce a simple calibration approach, Attend to Your Own Thoughts (AYOT), where reasoning traces and final answers generated by the pre-trained high-precision target LLM on a proper set of calibration samples are used as the context input during the ternarization process, along with the corresponding questions. ScaleQ-1.58 is formed by simply integrating AYOT with CAT-Q, which demonstrates several scaling properties: (1) with only 4M calibration tokens, Qwen3-1.7B ternarized by ScaleQ-1.58 reaches over 90.52% of the performance of the prior best BitNet b1.58 2B4T averaged over 4 mathematics and coding tasks, and our ternary Qwen3-4B shows an absolute gain of 8.97%, while requiring 1,000,000x fewer calibration tokens for quantization; (2) ScaleQ-1.58 generalizes well to both dense and MoE architectures, with performance improving as model scale increases (up to 235B parameters); (3) ScaleQ-1.58 demonstrates strong generalization across tasks of varying difficulty levels, including mathematics, coding and scientific logic reasoning, as well as commonsense reasoning and basic language generation; (4) its performance continues to improve as the number of calibration tokens increases. Notably, AYOT also exhibits strong generalization ability across other quantization bit-widths. Code will be available at https://github.com/IntelChina-AI/BitTern.

</details>


### [132] [Morphology Aware Reversible Semantic Tokenization and Hierarchical Word Composition for Tamil Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01153)
*Anand Murugan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个泰米尔语形态学系统，扩展了开源的ThamizhMorph分析器和生成器，包含一个逐字节精确的语义分词器和一个学习到的层次化词组成器。该系统通过12个有限状态转换器将单词分析为词元和语法特征，并在固定的小型模型预算下与多种分词器基线进行比较。实验表明，显式泰米尔语形态学在翻译质量上优于外部基线（最高提升7.2% BLEU），同时层次化词组成器将源端序列长度大幅缩短59.3%，推理FLOPs降低9-21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计子词分词器虽然能处理任意文本，但其切分单元不一定与词法或语法结构对齐。对于泰米尔语这种高度黏着语言尤为重要——一个书面词可能编码词干变化、格、数、时态、一致、语态、附着词和连接动词等多种语法信息。现有子词分词器缺乏对泰米尔语形态结构的显式建模，可能损害机器翻译质量。

Method: 扩展ThamizhMorph系统，使用12个有限状态转换器对单词进行词元和语法特征分析，提供字符和字节回退机制以保证精确重建。提出了两种分词方案：平面形态分词器（morphology-flat）和信号保持的层次化词组成器（word composer）。所有系统使用相同的69,591对泰米尔语-英语训练数据、18.97百万参数编码器-解码器、40,000次更新等设置进行公平对比。

Result: 在受保护的3,539行IN22和FLORES+评估集上，morphology-flat取得最佳综合分数：BLEU 10.63，chrF++ 35.26，COMETKiwi 0.6276，相对最强的外部基线AI4Bharat分别提升7.2%、3.2%和2.6%。词组成器得分10.30/34.88/0.6241，相对AI4Bharat提升3.8%/2.1%/2.0%。词组成器将平均全局源端状态从71.48降至29.08（减少59.3%），推理FLOPs估计减少9-21%；其质量差距主要集中在较长的FLORES+句子上。

Conclusion: 在固定的小型模型预算下，显式泰米尔语形态建模能显著提升机器翻译质量；层次化词组成器在大幅降低序列长度和估计推理成本的同时，仍能保持接近最优的翻译质量。

Abstract: Statistical subword tokenizers can process arbitrary text, but their units need not align with lexical or grammatical structure. This is especially important for Tamil, where a written word may encode stem changes, case, number, tense, agreement, voice, clitics, and linked verbs. We present a Tamil morphology system extending the open-source ThamizhiMorph analyzer and generator, together with a byte-exact semantic tokenizer and a learned hierarchical word composer. Twelve finite-state transducers analyze words into lemmas and grammatical features, while character and byte fallbacks preserve exact reconstruction.
  We compare a flat morphology tokenizer, a signal-preserving word composer, and tokenizers based on Sarvam-1, AI4Bharat IndicBERTv2, and BrahmicTokenizer-131K. All systems use the same 69,591 Tamil-English training pairs, 18.97-million-parameter encoder-decoder, 40,000 updates, target tokenizer, optimizer, positional method, and generation settings.
  On a protected 3,539-row IN22 and FLORES+ evaluation, morphology-flat achieves the best pooled scores: 10.63 BLEU, 35.26 chrF++, and 0.6276 COMETKiwi. Relative to AI4Bharat, the strongest external-tokenizer baseline, these are improvements of 7.2%, 3.2%, and 2.6%. The word composer scores 10.30, 34.88, and 0.6241, improving on AI4Bharat by 3.8%, 2.1%, and 2.0%.
  The composer reduces mean global source states from 71.48 to 29.08, a 59.3% reduction, and is estimated to require 9-21% fewer inference FLOPs depending on decoder caching. Its remaining quality gap is concentrated in longer FLORES+ sentences. These results show that explicit Tamil morphology improves translation under a fixed small-model budget, while hierarchical composition substantially reduces sequence length and estimated inference cost.

</details>


### [133] [PlainMedScale: A Corpus of Multi-Level Simplified Medical Texts in German and English](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01158)
*Bruno Brocai,Ilaria Papagno,Mayumi Ohta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PlainMedScale，一个覆盖德语和英语、横跨四个理解难度层级的医学主题对齐语料库，超越传统专家-外行二元对立，并通过对齐实现的两项初步研究表明：现有可读性指标在完整难度梯度上无法泛化，且SOTA开源LLM在提示生成简易语言时仍部分保留输入的难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学语料库仅区分专家与外行两个层级（二元对立），无法捕捉医患沟通中实际存在的多级理解需求。已有可读性指标建立在二元语料基础上，其在跨更多难度层级时的泛化能力未知；同时，对LLM在简易语言生成任务中是否真正降低文本难度也缺乏系统评估。

Method: 构建PlainMedScale语料库：从MSD（专业人士版与消费者版）、Gesund.Bund、Apotheken Umschau Einfache Sprache和NHS等来源收集德语与英语医学文本，按四个理解层级（参考、解释、决策支持、获取）进行主题对齐。以此为基础开展两项初步研究：(1) 评估已有可读性指标在四层级梯度上的推广性；(2) 测试SOTA开源LLM在提示生成简易语言时对输入难度的保持情况。

Result: 两项初步研究发现：许多在二元语料上建立的可读性指标无法在完整四层级难度梯度上良好泛化；SOTA开源LLM即使被明确提示生成易读文本（Plain Language），其输出仍部分保留了输入文本的难度特征。

Conclusion: PlainMedScale为医学文本可读性研究提供了更细粒度的多层级基准资源，揭示了现有可读性指标的局限性和LLM简易化生成的不足，为后续改进指标设计和LLM可读性控制提供了数据基础。

Abstract: We introduce PlainMedScale, a topic-aligned medical corpus spanning four levels of comprehensibility in German and English, drawn from MSD (professional and consumer), Gesund.Bund, Apotheken Umschau Einfache Sprache, and the NHS. The four tiers correspond to distinct communicative functions --- reference, explanation, decision support, and access --- and move beyond the binary expert--lay contrast of prior corpora. In two pilot studies enabled by the alignments, we show that many readability metrics established on two registers fail to generalize across the full gradient, and that a SOTA open-weight LLM prompted for Plain Language still partially preserves the difficulty of its input. Code (https://github.com/GS-Uni-Heidelberg/PlainMedScale) and data (https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.21728290) are made available.

</details>


### [134] [Does Machine "know" interpersonal pragmatics? Evidence from MARBERT's learning of emoji pragmatics in Arabic digital discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01174)
*Mohammed Q. Shormani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了基于Transformer的模型（MARBERT）在阿拉伯语数字话语中学习表情符号语用功能的能力，通过8,504条手工标注的Facebook帖子，针对五种人际语用功能（礼貌、尊重、团结、共情、鼓励）进行微调，取得了93%的准确率，但F1分数较低（微F1=0.61，宏F1=0.56），表明模型能学习面子管理和关系沟通模式，但在高度隐含的社会意义上仍有挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的情感分析无法捕捉表情符号在数字话语中复杂的人际语用功能，尤其是在阿拉伯语这一资源稀缺、语用丰富的语言环境中。作者旨在将人际语用学理论与NLP相结合，探索Transformer模型能否超越简单情感分析，学习更深层的语用意图。

Method: 采用混合方法：(1) 从Facebook收集8,504条唯一表情符号帖子作为语料库；(2) 手工标注五种人际语用功能（Politeness、Respect、Solidarity、Empathy、Encouragement）；(3) 基于言语行为理论、礼貌理论和人际关系管理理论进行解释性分析；(4) 使用统计方法与定性分析相结合；(5) 对MARBERT进行微调以建模上下文相关语用功能。

Result: MARBERT在未见数据上达到93%的准确率、0.61的微F1和0.56的宏F1。在功能级评估中，礼貌和尊重的识别准确率高于团结，反映了不同人际语用功能在显式性和上下文依赖性上的差异。

Conclusion: Transformer模型能够学习面子管理和关系沟通的模式，但在处理高度隐含的社会意义方面仍面临挑战。该研究为表情符号语用学建模提供了新的计算方法，推进了人际语用学与NLP在数字传播研究中的融合。

Abstract: This study examines Transformer-based models' ability to learn emoji pragmatics in Arabic digital discourse (ADD), providing evidence from MARBERT's behavior with interpersonal pragmatic functions (IPFs). A corpus of 8,504 unique emoji-posts collected from Facebook via Python was used in the study. These posts were manually annotated, developed, and labeled for five IPFs: Politeness, Respect, Solidarity, Empathy, and Encouragement. A mixed-method approach was employed comprising statistical methods and interpretative analyses involving speech act theory, politeness theory, and rapport management theory. MARBERT was fine-tuned to model these context-dependent pragmatic functions. Findings demonstrate MARBERT's ability to learn these IPFs, achieving strong performance on unseen data, with an accuracy of 93%, a micro F1-score of 0.61, and a macro F1-score of 0.56, demonstrating its effectiveness in capturing interpersonal functions beyond conventional sentiment analysis. Function-level evaluation showed that Politeness and Respect were identified more accurately than Solidarity, reflecting differences in the explicitness and contextual dependence of IPFs. The study concludes that Transformer-based models learn patterns of face management and relational communication but remain challenged by highly implicit social meanings. It contributes a novel computational approach to modeling emoji pragmatics and advances the integration of interpersonal pragmatics with NLP for digital communication research.

</details>


### [135] [When Words Divide: Diachronic Ideological Polarization in Political Discourse on Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01176)
*Roy Yitzchak,Noa Lavie,Ella Rabinovich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对Reddit讨论中的意识形态极化进行了纵向分析，通过构建时间对齐的社区特定词嵌入，测量对立政治社区在语言上的语义分歧，从而量化意识形态极化随时间的变化。研究发现极化在研究期间显著增加，为大规模社交媒体话语中意识形态极化的时间动态研究提供了可扩展的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政治极化已成为在线话语的标志性特征，但其长期演化过程仍未被充分理解。已有计算研究工作大多集中于横截面分析或在单一时间点上衡量极化的情感维度，无法捕捉意识形态差异在语义框架上的演变。

Method: 构建时间对齐的社区特定词嵌入向量，将政治概念的语义分歧量化为意识形态极化指标，在概念层面和主题层面分别进行衡量，从而追踪极化随时间的动态变化。

Result: 分析表明，在研究期间，意识形态极化在概念层面和主题层面均显著增加。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架提供了一种可扩展的方法，用于研究大规模社交媒体话语中意识形态极化的时间动态，弥补了以往研究中对极化演化过程关注的不足。

Abstract: Political polarization has become a defining feature of online discourse, yet its long-term evolution remains poorly understood. We present a longitudinal analysis of ideological polarization in Reddit discussions by measuring semantic differences in the language used by opposing political communities. We construct temporally aligned community-specific word embeddings and quantify ideological polarization as the semantic divergence of political concepts over time. Our analysis shows that ideological polarization has increased substantially during the study period, both at the concept- and topic-level. Unlike prior computational work, which has largely focused on cross-sectional analyses or affective dimensions of polarization at a single point at time, our approach captures the evolution of ideological differences in semantic framing. The proposed framework provides a scalable method for studying the temporal dynamics of ideological polarization in large-scale social media discourse.

</details>


### [136] [ShiJianBench: From Dialogue to Decision for Long-Horizon Evaluation of Investment Advisors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01204)
*Jie Gong,Maowei Jiang,Zhiwei Liu,Yang Qiao,Wenxi Wu,Mengxi Xiao,Enze Zhang,Ziyan Kuang,Yankai Chen,Caishuang Huang,Meng Zhou,Xiku Du,Xue Liu,Guojun Xiong,Min Peng,Qianqian Xie,Sophia Ananiadou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ShiJianBench，一个通过匹配投资者轨迹在固定历史市场反馈下评估对话式投资顾问的离线框架，包含多智能体投资者模拟器，并在2021-2026年中国基金市场数据上识别出稳定的领先LLM顾问群体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估主要衡量回复质量或观测结果，难以审计从顾问语言到投资者行为的长期影响路径，缺乏对对话式投资顾问长期干预效果的评估工具。

Method: 构建离线评估框架ShiJianBench，核心是多智能体投资者模拟器，包含显式演化状态变量、动机驱动的推理、长期记忆和对话接地的更新机制；模拟器基于7,199名真实用户的聚合行为模式进行校准；评估采用投资者侧、服务侧和内容侧三类指标，并设置硬性合规门槛。

Result: 在2021-2026年中国基金市场数据上的实验识别出稳定的领先LLM顾问群体，这些顾问在个性化内容方面显著更强，同时在投资者侧轨迹结果上具有竞争力；揭示了生成高质量回复与实现有效长期干预之间的系统性差异。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明需要轨迹感知的对话式顾问评估方法，将长期行为轨迹纳入评估标准，而非仅关注单次回复质量或短期结果。

Abstract: Conversational investment advisors influence not only what users know, but also how they make subsequent decisions as market conditions evolve. Existing evaluations primarily assess response quality or observed outcomes, leaving the long-horizon pathway from advisor language to investor behavior difficult to audit. We introduce ShiJianBench, an offline framework for evaluating conversational investment advisors through matched investor trajectories under fixed historical market feedback. At its core is a multi-agent investor simulator with explicit evolving state variables, motive-driven deliberation, long-term memory, and dialogue-grounded updates. The simulator is calibrated against aggregate behavioral patterns from 7,199 real users, and advisor policies are evaluated using separate investor-side, service-side, and content-side metrics under a hard compliance gate. Experiments on Chinese fund-market traces from 2021 to 2026 identify a stable leading group of LLM advisors that combines substantially stronger personalized content with competitive investor-side trajectory outcomes. These results reveal a systematic distinction between producing a high-quality response and delivering an effective long-horizon intervention, motivating trajectory-aware evaluation of conversational advisors.

</details>


### [137] [Evaluating VLMs on Multimodal Aristotelian Persuasion Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01238)
*Khondoker Ittehadul Islam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了视觉语言模型（VLMs）在复杂的说服力分析任务上的表现，使用ImageArg数据集对Logos、Ethos和Pathos三种修辞说服检测任务进行评测，发现Qwen系列模型表现优异，Qwen3在Logos和Pathos任务上表现突出，Qwen2在更复杂的Ethos检测任务上具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然视觉语言模型（VLMs）在多种任务上表现出色，但在更复杂的任务上的评估尚不充分。亚里士多德提出的说服模型（Persuasion Model）呈三角形结构，涉及个人偏见相关挑战，作者希望评估VLMs在这些复杂说服任务上的进展。

Method: 使用ImageArg数据集，聚焦于Logos（逻辑）、Ethos（可信度）、Pathos（情感）三种修辞说服检测任务，对多个视觉语言模型进行系统性评估，特别关注Qwen家族模型的表现，并发布代码以促进该方向的研究。

Result: Qwen系列的模型在F1分数上表现提升，其中Qwen3在Logos和Pathos两个任务上表现尤为出色，而Qwen2在更为复杂的Ethos检测任务上展现出较强的竞争力。

Conclusion: VLMs在复杂说服力分析任务上已展现出一定的能力，但不同模型在不同修辞维度上的优势各异。作者发布了代码以推动该研究方向的进一步发展。

Abstract: Vision Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated exceptional performance across various tasks. However, they have not yet been thoroughly evaluated on more complex tasks. The Persuasion Model, conceived by Aristotle, resembles a triangle shape, which highlights its inherent challenges related to personal biases. To assess the progress of VLMs on these complex tasks, we use the ImageArg datasets, focusing on the Logos, Ethos, and Pathos detection tasks. Our findings indicate that models from the Qwen family achieve improved F1 scores, with Qwen3 performing exceptionally well on the Logos and Pathos tasks, while Qwen2 exhibits competitive performance on the more complex Ethos detection task. We release the code to foster research in this direction.

</details>


### [138] [DeltaFlow: Noise-Adaptive Bidirectional Gated Delta Networks for Embedded Language Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01240)
*Guangfu Guo,Xiaoqian Lu,Linsey Pang,Weiran Yao,Haolin Chen,Kunpeng Liu,Long Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DeltaFlow 是一种噪声自适应的双向门控Delta网络（Gated Delta Network）骨干架构，用于连续语言去噪任务。它通过交替扫描方向（DeltaFlow-A）或层内并行前后向扫描（DeltaFlow-P）替代全非因果注意力的二次复杂度序列混合成本，并引入噪声自适应记忆控制和调度式时间状态一致性（TSC）来稳定隐藏表示。在OpenWebText上，DeltaFlow-P相比全注意力ELF基线将生成困惑度从24.218降至21.228，在16k序列长度下实现2.72倍吞吐量加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式语言流（ELF）依赖全非因果注意力进行迭代去噪，每一步采样都反复付出二次方序列混合成本，计算效率低下。虽然门控Delta网络（GDN）提供了高效循环替代方案，但其标准因果形式无法捕捉ELF所需的双向上下文。因此需要一个既能保持GDN效率又能提供双向上下文建模能力的去噪骨干架构。

Method: 提出DeltaFlow，构建噪声自适应的双向GDN骨干。包含两个变体：DeltaFlow-A在层间交替扫描方向；DeltaFlow-P在每层内并行执行前向和后向扫描。引入噪声自适应记忆控制（根据噪声水平动态调整记忆状态）以及调度式时间状态一致性（TSC）来稳定邻近噪声水平之间的隐藏表示。

Result: 在OpenWebText上使用32步随机微分方程采样器，DeltaFlow-P将生成困惑度从全注意力ELF基线的24.218降至21.228，同时保持相当的一元熵；训练token暴露量为36B，少于基线的45B。在仅去噪器基准测试中，DeltaFlow-P在16k序列长度下实现2.72倍于全注意力基线的吞吐量加速。

Conclusion: DeltaFlow证明了双向门控Delta网络能够有效替代密集注意力，在连续语言去噪中不仅提升生成质量（更低的困惑度）和训练效率（更少的token暴露），还显著降低了推理计算成本，是ELF类方法的高效替代方案。

Abstract: Embedded Language Flows (ELF) rely primarily on full non-causal attention for iterative denoising, repeatedly incurring quadratic sequence-mixing cost at each sampling step. Gated Delta Networks (GDNs) provide an efficient recurrent alternative, but their standard causal formulation cannot directly capture the bidirectional context required by ELF. We introduce DeltaFlow, a noise-adaptive bidirectional GDN backbone for continuous language denoising. We study two variants: DeltaFlow-A, which alternates scan directions across layers, and DeltaFlow-P, which performs parallel forward and backward scans within each layer. We further introduce noise-adaptive memory control and scheduled Temporal State Consistency (TSC) to stabilize hidden representations across nearby noise levels. On OpenWebText, using a 32-step stochastic differential equation sampler, DeltaFlow-P reduces generated perplexity from 24.218 for the full-attention ELF baseline to 21.228 while maintaining comparable unigram entropy, with 36B training-token exposure compared with 45B for the baseline. In a denoiser-only benchmark, DeltaFlow-P achieves a 2.72x throughput speedup over the full-attention baseline at a sequence length of 16k. These results show that DeltaFlow is a promising alternative to dense attention for efficient continuous language denoising.

</details>


### [139] [RestoreKV: Recovering Full-Cache Behavior Under Aggressive Query-Agnostic KV Cache Eviction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01247)
*Changwoo Baek,Seungjun Shin,Kyeongbo Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RestoreKV，一种为查询无关的KV缓存淘汰方法补充"学习式恢复"的机制。在上下文预填充后，通过单次LoRA适配的注意力传递，让少量恢复token关注完整KV缓存，生成紧凑的、上下文条件化的恢复缓存。基础重要性打分器和淘汰规则不变，适配器在后续查询和解码中被禁用。通过参数高效的自我蒸馏从冻结的全缓存模型训练，仅优化0.4%的参数，无需任务特定微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 查询无关的KV缓存淘汰方法在压缩上下文后可复用于任意未来查询，但在紧凑预算下性能可能崩溃。现有方法主要改进保留哪些原始KV对，但在极端压缩下仍无法避免信息丢失。

Method: 1) 在上下文预填充后，引入少量"恢复token"通过单次LoRA适配的注意力传递关注完整KV缓存，生成紧凑的上下文条件化恢复缓存；2) 基础重要性评分器和淘汰规则保持不变，适配器在后续查询与解码阶段被禁用；3) 通过参数高效的自我蒸馏，从冻结的全缓存模型学习，仅优化0.4%的参数。

Result: 在四个骨干模型和四个长上下文基准上，RestoreKV显著降低压缩引起的性能退化。在Qwen3-4B上，59/60个配对预算匹配设置优于五种基础淘汰方法；5%预算下，在RULER-4K上将KVzip从38.2提升至73.2。应用于KVzip+时，在KVPress Benchmark上16倍压缩达到86.4的RULER准确率，同时32K上下文评估中一次性缓存构建开销增加不到0.5%。

Conclusion: RestoreKV通过在学习式恢复与缓存淘汰之间形成互补，在保持查询无关性和极低开销的前提下，大幅提升了紧凑预算下KV缓存压缩的性能，且无需任务特定微调。

Abstract: Query-agnostic KV cache eviction compresses a context once and reuses the resulting cache for arbitrary future queries, but performance can collapse under tight budgets. Existing methods primarily improve which original KV pairs are retained. We introduce RestoreKV, which complements this selection-based formulation with learned restoration under the same total KV budget. Our key insight is that, although the information lost through eviction is context-specific, the mechanism for generating its compact complement can be shared across contexts. After context prefill, a few restore tokens attend to the full KV cache in a single LoRA-adapted pass, generating a compact, context-conditioned restore cache. The base importance scorer and eviction rule remain unchanged, and the adapters are disabled for all subsequent queries and decoding. RestoreKV is trained through parameter-efficient self-distillation from the frozen full-cache model, optimizing only $0.4\%$ of the parameters and requiring no task-specific tuning. Across four backbones and four long-context benchmarks, RestoreKV substantially reduces compression-induced degradation. On Qwen3-4B, it improves 59 of 60 paired, budget-matched settings across five base eviction methods; at a $5\%$ budget, it raises KVzip from $38.2$ to $73.2$ on RULER-4K. Applied to KVzip+, RestoreKV reaches $86.4$ RULER accuracy at $16\times$ compression on the KVPress Benchmark, while adding less than $0.5\%$ one-time cache-construction overhead in a 32K-context evaluation. Our project page is available at https://paper.pnu-cvsp.com/RestoreKV/

</details>


### [140] [ACE-GraphRAG: Agentic Context Engineering for Hierarchical GraphRAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01269)
*Yongfeng Huang,Yuren Lai,Ruiying Chen,Haoyu Huang,Mingming Zhao,James Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ACE-GraphRAG，一种用于层级GraphRAG的推理时上下文策略层，通过间隙感知的精细化检索、并行差分检索和任务条件自适应来弥合"表示-推理鸿沟"，在多跳QA和查询聚焦摘要任务上超越现有RAG和GraphRAG基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层级GraphRAG以多粒度组织语料知识，但固定的上下文构建方式无法将多分辨率表示转化为适应当前查询的上下文，导致"表示-推理鸿沟"问题。

Method: ACE-GraphRAG将上下文构建形式化为一个策略，包含三个核心组件：(1) 间隙感知的精细化（gap-aware refinement）；(2) 并行差分检索（Parallel Differential Retrieval），从深度导向的事实分支和广度导向的语义分支获取补充证据；(3) 任务条件适应（task-conditioned adaptation）。提供两种变体：Full-ACE（在任务族内统一应用完整策略）和Adaptive-ACE（为每条查询选择任务和拓扑特定策略）。检索到的证据增量与初始上下文合并时保持来源和抽象层级。

Result: 在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA和四个UltraDomain子集上评估：Full-ACE在多跳QA和查询聚焦摘要两个任务族上均优于所评估的RAG和GraphRAG基线；Adaptive-ACE进一步提升了多跳QA性能，且在全部四个UltraDomain子集上被偏好于Full-ACE。消融和拓扑分析支持将上下文构建视为查询和任务依赖的推理策略而非固定过程。

Conclusion: 将上下文构建视为查询和任务相关的推理策略而非固定流程是更优的设计选择。ACE-GraphRAG通过策略化、差分化和自适应的上下文工程有效弥合了层级GraphRAG中多分辨率表示与生成推理之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Hierarchical Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GraphRAG) organizes corpus knowledge at multiple levels of granularity, yet fixed context construction may fail to translate these multi-resolution representations into a context suited to the current query. We identify this mismatch as the representation--inference gap. We propose Agentic Context Engineering for Hierarchical GraphRAG (ACE-GraphRAG), an inference-time context policy layer that supplements and adapts the initial context for generation. ACE-GraphRAG formulates context construction as a policy over gap-aware refinement, retrieval branches, and task-conditioned adaptation. Parallel Differential Retrieval acquires supplementary evidence from depth-oriented factual and breadth-oriented semantic branches. These evidence increments are consolidated with the initial context while preserving provenance and abstraction levels. Full-ACE applies the full policy uniformly within each task family, whereas Adaptive-ACE selects task- and topology-specific policies for individual queries. We evaluate ACE-GraphRAG on HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and four UltraDomain subsets across multi-hop QA and query-focused summarization. Full-ACE outperforms the evaluated RAG and GraphRAG baselines across both task families, while Adaptive-ACE further improves multi-hop QA and is preferred over Full-ACE on all four UltraDomain subsets. Ablation and topology analyses support treating context construction as a query- and task-dependent inference policy rather than a fixed procedure.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [ParticleGen: A Multi-Agent System for Particle Effects Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00629)
*Junhao Zhuge,Junyi Yang,Yuqing Wang,Kangzhan Wang,Sipeng Yang,Xiaogang Jin*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多智能体框架，能够从自然语言描述中从零合成结构化、可编辑的粒子系统。框架通过解耦的规划和参数化流程生成初始粒子配置，并基于渲染反馈迭代优化，同时引入诊断机制将视觉伪影关联到底层程序原因。在UE5 Niagara系统中验证了该方法在元素法术、自然现象、烟花等场景的高语义保真度和视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子系统在数字娱乐中广泛使用，但高质量的粒子效果创作既费时又需要专业知识，要求创作者处理复杂的程序规则和高维参数空间。虽然大语言模型支持用户用自然语言描述粒子效果，但从高层创作意图可靠地转换为可执行的程序逻辑和底层参数仍然困难。

Method: 提出一个多智能体框架，包括：(1) 解耦的规划与参数化流水线，从文本提示生成初始粒子配置；(2) 基于渲染反馈的迭代优化机制；(3) 诊断机制，将观察到的视觉伪影（artifacts）关联到其底层的程序原因，以支持精确、有针对性的调整。

Result: 在Unreal Engine 5的Niagara系统中，跨多种场景（包括元素法术、动态自然现象和烟花）进行了验证。定量和定性评估显示该方法实现了高语义保真度和视觉质量。

Conclusion: 通过直接合成结构化的粒子模拟逻辑，该框架降低了粒子效果创作的技术门槛，提升了创作迭代的效率。

Abstract: Particle systems are widely used in digital entertainment to create dynamic scene elements and visual effects. However, authoring high-quality particle effects remains labor-intensive and demands specialized expertise, requiring practitioners to navigate complex procedural rules and high-dimensional parameter spaces. Recent large language models (LLMs) enable users to specify particle effects through natural language, yet reliably translating high-level creative intent into executable procedural logic and low-level parameters remains difficult. In this work, we present a multi-agent framework for the from-scratch synthesis of structured and editable particle systems from natural language descriptions. Given a text prompt, our framework first generates an initial particle configuration through a decoupled planning and parameterization pipeline, and then iteratively improves the result based on rendered feedback. To support precise and targeted adjustments, we further introduce a diagnostic mechanism that links observed visual artifacts to their underlying procedural causes. We validate our approach in Unreal Engine 5's Niagara system across a diverse set of scenarios, including elemental spells, dynamic natural phenomena, and fireworks. Quantitative and qualitative evaluations show that our method achieves high semantic fidelity and visual quality. By directly synthesizing structured particle simulation logic, our framework reduces the technical barrier to particle effect authoring and improves the efficiency of creative iteration.

</details>


### [142] [Hi-TOPS: Hierarchical Topology-aware Scoring Prior for 3D Part Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00767)
*Ruoyu Wu,Zhenhong Sun,Xiaoming Gong,Yuxin Xian,Zhi Wang,Yawen Chen,Huadong Mo,Daoyi Dong*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: Hi-TOPS提出一种层次化拓扑感知评分先验，通过多分辨率Flow-Freeze场聚合互补的内在线索，实现无需语义监督或2D基础先验的准确3D部件分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D部件分解方法存在结构尺度不匹配问题：分离的几何证据在中尺度最可靠，但许多管线要么过于全局化（无法保留关节），要么过于局部化（对噪声不鲁棒），导致难以准确处理关节接缝和薄连接结构。

Method: 将互补的内在线索聚合成多分辨率Flow-Freeze场，其中Flow区域为原始部件覆盖提供可扩展支持，Freeze区域在关节和薄结构附近限制生长；然后利用TSDF引导的体表超二次曲面拟合器捕获主导核心和残余表面结构，最后通过SQ到网格的分配生成连通、边界对齐的部件。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，Hi-TOPS在无需语义监督或2D基础先验的条件下，稳定地产生了可编辑的部件分解结果。

Conclusion: Hi-TOPS通过层次化拓扑感知策略有效解决了结构尺度不匹配问题，能够在不依赖外部监督的情况下实现准确且可编辑的3D部件分解。

Abstract: Accurate 3D part decomposition requires separating shapes into structurally meaningful components with precise boundaries while preserving articulation seams and thin attachments. Existing approaches often suffer from a structural-scale mismatch: geometric evidence for separation is most reliable at the meso scale, yet many pipelines operate either too globally to respect joints or too locally to remain robust to noise. We propose Hi-TOPS, a Hierarchical Topology-aware Scoring Prior that aggregates complementary intrinsic cues into a multi-resolution Flow-Freeze field. Flow regions provide expandable support for primitive coverage, while Freeze regions restrict growth near articulations and thin structures. A TSDF-guided body-surface superquadric fitter then captures dominant cores and residual surface structures, followed by SQ-to-mesh assignment for connected, boundary-aligned parts. Across diverse benchmarks, Hi-TOPS delivers stable, editable decompositions without semantic supervision or 2D foundation priors.

</details>


### [143] [ETHead: Generating Expressive 3D Facial Animation and Head Movement from Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01605)
*Jiu-Cheng Xie,Jiwang Zheng,Yongkang Xia,Jian Xiong,Chi-Man Pun,Hao Gao,Feng Xu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出ETHead方法，利用大规模2D说话视频数据通过自蒸馏框架训练专用语音编码器，并结合情感调制概率掩码机制对齐语音表征与表达性视觉动态，从而生成与输入语音情感内容生动匹配的3D面部和头部运动。实验表明该方法显著优于现有技术，且编码器可迁移到其他3D说话头框架中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真3D数据稀缺限制了复杂情感运动模式的建模，导致仅从语音生成富有表现力的3D说话头仍是一大挑战。

Method: 设计自蒸馏框架，利用大规模2D说话视频预训练专用语音编码器；引入情感调制概率掩码机制对齐语音表征与表达性视觉动态；通过联合语音-运动潜空间提供显式监督以指导3D生成。

Result: 大量实验表明ETHead在多个指标上显著优于现有最先进方法，且语音编码器可作为可迁移模块增强其他3D说话头动画框架的表现力。

Conclusion: ETHead通过充分利用2D视频数据缓解3D数据稀缺问题，实现了与语音情感内容保持一致的高表现力3D面部和头部运动生成，并提供了可泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: Generating expressive 3D talking heads solely from speech remains a significant challenge due to the scarcity of high-fidelity 3D data, which limits the modeling of complex emotional motion patterns. In this paper, we introduce \textbf{E}xpressive \textbf{T}alking \textbf{Head} (ETHead), a method for generating 3D facial and head motions that vividly align with the emotional content of input speech. To overcome the data limitations, we design a self-distillation framework that leverages large-scale 2D talking videos to pre-train a specialized speech encoder. By incorporating a novel emotion-modulated probabilistic masking mechanism, this framework aligns speech representations with expressive visual dynamics, allowing the encoder to extract features highly correlated with facial and head motions directly from audio. These features are then leveraged to guide 3D generation, enriching input cues and providing explicit supervision through a joint speech-motion latent space. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ETHead substantially outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Furthermore, our motion-aligned speech encoder can serve as a transferable module, offering a general solution for enhancing expressiveness in other 3D talking head animation frameworks. The project page is available at https://verdure-oss.github.io/ETHead.github.io/.

</details>


### [144] [High-fidelity tabletop nanoscopy enabled by non-linear spectral preconditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01746)
*Yun Xie,Zhiyi Huang,Bianli Zhao,Youyang Zhou,Steve F. Shu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文针对实验室尺度光源下X射线叠层成像（Ptychography）中探测器位深限制导致的高动态范围（HDR）成像瓶颈，提出了一种多尺度非线性融合方法，突破传统HDR方法对辐射度线性度的严格要求，在无需严格线性假设的前提下实现高保真重建，兼顾噪声抑制与物理保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叠层成像在同步辐射设施中已常规应用，但移植到实验室光源时受限于低光子通量。宽动态范围的衍射信号与探测器位深限制构成关键瓶颈，难以同时记录低频强度和高频细节。现有HDR成像方法强制要求辐射度线性度（融合强度与波前模平方成正比），以符合泊松似然模型，但这种严格线性假设在实际实验条件下难以满足。

Method: 提出一种多尺度非线性融合方法，将其引入叠层成像流程中。该方法放弃了对融合强度严格线性的前提要求，改用多尺度非线性策略融合不同动态范围的衍射信号，在重建过程中无需依赖线性辐射度假设即可实现准确重构。

Result: 该方法缓解了传统方法在噪声抑制与物理保真度之间的固有权衡，在强色散条件下仍能实现鲁棒成像，并显著拓宽了有效光谱带宽。

Conclusion: 研究表明严格的辐射度线性并非叠层成像准确重建的必要条件，多尺度非线性融合为实验室尺度光源下的高动态范围叠层成像提供了新的可行路径，扩大了其应用范围。

Abstract: Ptychography is a powerful lensless imaging technique that overcomes conventional numerical aperture limits to achieve diffraction-limited resolution. While routine at high-brilliance synchrotron facilities, its application to laboratory-scale sources is primarily limited by low photon flux. Under these conditions, the wide dynamic range of diffraction signals presents a critical bottleneck where detector bit-depth limitations hinder the simultaneous recording of low-frequency intensity and high-frequency details. Currently, most high-dynamic-range (HDR) imaging methods enforce strict radiometric linearity, assuming the fused intensity must be linearly proportional to the squared modulus of the wavefront to satisfy Poisson likelihood models. In this paper, we introduce a multi-scale non-linear fusion approach into the ptychographic pipeline, demonstrating that strict linearity is not a prerequisite for accurate reconstruction. This method mitigates the traditional trade-off between noise suppression and physical fidelity, enables robust imaging under strong dispersion, and significantly broadens the effective spectral bandwidth.

</details>


### [145] [Breaking optoelectronic SNR limitations via physics-consistent computational diffractive imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01757)
*Yun Xie,Bianli Zhao,Han Yue,Rui Zhang,Zhiyi Huang,Weiran Jiang,Chuangchuang Cheng,Steve F. Shu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出将探测器信息感知的测量一致性引入叠层衍射成像（ptychography）重建中，通过标定像素级传感器响应构建空间分辨的置信度图，并将其嵌入迭代振幅约束，从而对不可靠的探测器残差进行降权，保留物理上有意义的衍射信息。实验表明该方法在透射、反射及弱生物相位成像中实现了约两倍信噪比提升，重建接近瑞利极限（k因子约0.65）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叠层衍射成像在光子受限条件下，微弱的高频衍射信号常与空间异质的探测器噪声重叠，而大多数重建算法仍将探测器视为理想测量平面，忽视了非理想检测硬件对成像性能的制约。

Method: 通过标定像素级传感器响应构建空间分辨的置信度图，将其嵌入迭代振幅约束中，使不可靠的探测器残差被降权处理，同时保留物理上有意义的衍射信息，实现探测器信息感知的测量一致性约束。

Result: 在透射、反射和弱生物相位成像实验中，衍射数据质量提升，信噪比约提升两倍，重建接近瑞利极限，测得k因子约0.65。与先进的去噪方法相比，该方法在抑制探测器诱导背景与保留结构衍射信息之间取得了更好的平衡。

Conclusion: 探测器可靠性可以作为闭环物理约束来扩展不完美传感器下叠层衍射成像的性能极限。

Abstract: Ptychography is a powerful lensless imaging technique capable of approaching the diffraction limit, yet its performance is increasingly constrained by non-ideal detection hardware. In photon-limited measurements, weak high-frequency diffraction signals often overlap with spatially heterogeneous detector noise, whereas most reconstruction algorithms still treat the detector as an ideal measurement plane. Here, we introduce detector-informed measurement consistency into ptychographic reconstruction. By calibrating the pixelwise sensor response, the method construct a spatially resolved confidence map and embed it into the iterative amplitude constraint, allowing unreliable detector residuals to be down-weighted while preserving physically meaningful diffraction information. Experiments across transmission, reflection, and weak biological phase imaging show improved diffraction-data quality, an approximately twofold signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) enhancement, and reconstruction approaching the Rayleigh limit with a measured (k)-factor of about 0.65. Compared with previous advanced denoising methods, the proposed framework achieves a better balance between suppressing detector-induced background and preserving structural diffraction information. These results show that detector reliability can be used as an in-loop physical constraint to extend the performance of ptychographic imaging with imperfect sensors.

</details>


### [146] [SNAP-tFDP: Massively Scalable Graph Layouts via Sparse Negative Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01907)
*Xin Chen,Shuowei Hou,Yifan Wang,Mingliang Xue,Zezheng Feng,Oliver Deussen,Weidong Huang,Yunhai Wang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于负采样的力导向布局（FDP）算法，以O(|E|)时间复杂度和低内存开销实现大规模图的布局，无需复杂多级表示。通过线性归一化度数加权与短程有界t分布力结合增强聚类分离，采用边中心负采样策略重建全局度数加权目标，并设计无锁、基于束的并行化方案。在12个大规模图上验证了其优于现有算法，内存平均降低72%，可在10秒内为400万节点/3400万边的图生成高质量布局。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 力导向布局（FDP）是大规模网络可视化的常用方法，但扩展到海量图时面临两大挑战：一是现有近似方法依赖辅助数据结构（如空间树），带来显著内存开销；二是传统幂函数力难以有效分离密集簇。因此需要一种既能保持低内存占用、又能清晰分离社区结构的高效布局算法。

Method: 1）提出线性归一化度数加权方案，结合短程有界t分布力，有效解开密集结构并增强视觉聚类分离；2）引入边中心负采样策略，自然重建全局度数加权目标函数，实现O(|E|)时间复杂度；3）设计无锁、束式（bundle-based）并行化方案，利用随机更新的稀疏性实现显著加速并减少访问冲突；4）支持GPU简单并行化。

Result: 在12个大规模图上的全面评估表明，该方法在邻域保持和聚类分离方面优于现有最先进算法。相比现有基线，内存消耗平均降低72%；利用GPU并行，可在10秒内为包含400万节点和3400万边的图生成高质量布局。

Conclusion: 所提出的负采样力导向布局算法在不依赖复杂多级表示和辅助空间结构的前提下，实现了大规模图的高效布局与清晰的社区结构可视化，在时间、内存和布局质量上均具有显著优势。

Abstract: Force-Directed Placement (FDP) is a widely used approach for network visualization, yet scaling it to massive graphs while preserving clear community structures remains a major computational and visual challenge. Existing approximation methods often rely on auxiliary data structures (e.g., spatial trees), which introduce substantial memory overhead; furthermore, traditional power-function-based forces frequently fail to separate dense clusters effectively. In this paper, we present a negative sampling-based algorithm that achieves O(|E|) time complexity with a low memory footprint, without requiring complex multi-level representations. In a first step, we introduce a linearly normalized degree-weighting scheme, which, combined with short-range bounded $t$-distribution forces, effectively untangles dense structures and enhances visual cluster separation. To optimize for this formulation efficiently, we introduce an edge-centric negative sampling strategy that naturally reconstructs the global degree-weighted objective. Furthermore, we design a lock-free, bundle-based parallelization scheme that leverages the sparsity of stochastic updates to achieve significant speedups while mitigating access conflicts. Comprehensive evaluations on 12 large-scale graphs demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms state-of-the-art algorithms in neighborhood preservation and cluster separation. Compared to existing baselines, our method reduces memory consumption by 72% on average and leverages simple GPU parallelism to generate a high-quality layout for a graph with 4 million nodes and 34 million edges in below 10 seconds.

</details>


### [147] [Fourier-Latent Diffusion for Constrained Generation of Triply Periodic Minimal Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02151)
*Shu Yan,Bohan Wang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散模型的TPMS（三周期极小曲面）可控生成框架，通过构建超过1.8万个独特TPMS的大规模数据集，将曲面投影到具有周期性和D2h对称性约束的紧凑Fourier潜在空间，并训练基于Transformer的扩散模型，支持无条件采样、确定性反演、局部编辑和条件生成，能够生成低平均曲率且满足稀疏几何约束和均质化线弹性性质的TPMS结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TPMS生成方法通常局限于少数典型族，或者生成的近似结构与精确极小性存在偏差，缺乏能够支持大规模可控生成和逆设计的方法。因此需要一种能够生成多样、低曲率且满足设计约束的TPMS通用框架。

Method: (1) 通过枚举可镜像基本边界体上的容许边界环并求解多样极小曲面片，构建超过18K个独特TPMS的大规模数据集；(2) 将每个曲面投影到紧凑的Fourier潜在空间，显式强制周期性和D2h对称性；(3) 在该潜在空间中训练基于Transformer的扩散模型，支持无条件采样、确定性反演、局部编辑以及用户指定约束下的条件生成。

Result: 实验表明，该模型能够生成多样化、低曲率的TPMS候选结构；在条件生成下，能够满足稀疏几何约束并匹配目标均质化线弹性性质。

Conclusion: 该框架为TPMS逆设计提供了一种实用工具，在满足几何和材料属性约束的前提下实现了多样化的TPMS生成，解决了现有方法在多样性和精确极小性方面的局限。

Abstract: We propose a diffusion-based generative framework for controllable generation of triply periodic minimal surface (TPMS) structures with low residual mean curvature.Existing TPMS generation approaches are often restricted to a small set of canonical families or produce TPMS-like approximations that deviate from exact minimality. To enable this generative framework, we first construct a large-scale dataset of over 18K unique TPMS by enumerating admissible boundary loops on mirrorable fundamental bounding volumes and solving for diverse minimal-surface patches. Each surface is then projected onto a compact Fourier latent space that explicitly enforces periodicity and $D_{2h}$ symmetry. Next, a transformer-based diffusion model is trained in this latent space to support unconditional sampling, deterministic inversion, local editing, and conditional generation under user-specified constraints. Experiments demonstrate that the model generates diverse, low-curvature TPMS candidates that, under conditioning, satisfy sparse geometric constraints and match target homogenized linear elastic properties, providing a practical tool for TPMS inverse design.

</details>


### [148] [PTI: A Hierarchical Symbolic Color Addressing System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02460)
*Hector M. Trevino*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出PTI（持久遍历身份），一种基于RGB颜色立方体递归二分划分的分层符号寻址系统。与标准分层颜色处理系统在计算完成后丢弃遍历路径不同，PTI将遍历路径形式化为持久符号标识符。每个8位通道RGB颜色被表示为有限的八进制数字序列（{0,...,7}），每个数字编码一个细分层级的八分体选择。PTI在结构上等价于Morton（Z-order）码的序列解释版本，其构造算法即经典八叉树量化的遍历过程。PTI不引入新颜色空间，也不声称改进现有基础方法，其贡献在于提供一种同时具备机器精度和分层可解释性的颜色表示，从地址结构可直接揭示颜色的家族谱系。该8位地址可无损解码为RGB，并可作为有名称的加权颜色方向序列进行组合式阅读。其明确定义的前缀语义支持直接地址操作，适用于颜色目录组织、基于截断的图像处理例程和颜色结构化探索。此外，简单地对地址逐位旋转即可生成结构和谐的颜色调色板，全程不离开RGB色域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准分层颜色处理系统通过递归空间划分组织颜色，但在计算完成后会丢弃遍历路径信息。本文旨在形式化保留该遍历路径作为持久符号标识符，从而在颜色表示中同时实现机器精度和分层可解释性。

Method: 通过对RGB颜色立方体进行递归二分（八叉树划分），将每个8位通道RGB颜色编码为有限序列的八进制数字（{0,...,7}），每个数字编码一个细分层级所选八分体。该构造算法基于经典八叉树量化（Gervautz和Purgathofer）的遍历过程，结构上等价于Morton Z-order码的序列形式。

Result: PTI提供了无损、可精确解码的RGB替代表示，地址本身可组合读取为加权颜色方向名称序列。前缀语义支持颜色目录组织、截断式图像处理和结构化颜色探索等操作，且简单的地址逐位旋转可生成基于RGB立方体几何的和谐调色板。

Conclusion: PTI的贡献不在于引入新颜色空间或改进现有量化方法，而在于提出一种兼具机器精确性与分层可解释性的颜色表示范式，使颜色的家族谱系从其地址结构中直接可读，并为后续操作提供了开放的结构化地址空间。

Abstract: This paper introduces PTI (Persistent Traversal Identity), a hierarchical symbolic addressing system for digital color derived from the recursive binary subdivision of the RGB color cube. Standard hierarchical color processing systems organize colors via recursive space partitioning but conventionally discard the traversal path once computation is complete. PTI formalizes the preservation of that traversal path as a persistent symbolic identifier. The resulting address is a finite sequence of eight-valued digits from the set {0, ..., 7}, each encoding the octant selected at one level of subdivision. Concatenated, they constitute a lossless alternative representation of any 8-bit-per-channel RGB color. PTI is structurally equivalent to the Morton (Z-order) code of a color interpreted as a sequence rather than an integer, and its construction algorithm is the octree traversal procedure of classical octree quantization, as established by Gervautz and Purgathofer. PTI does not introduce a new color space, nor does it claim to improve upon these foundational methods. Its contribution is a color representation that is simultaneously machine-precise and hierarchically interpretable, revealing a color's family lineage directly from its address structure. The same 8-digit address decodes losslessly to RGB and reads compositionally as a named sequence of weighted color directions. PTI's well-defined prefix semantics support direct address operations suited to color catalog organization, truncation-based image-processing routines, and structured exploration of colors. The address structure invites further operations beyond this. A simple one, successively rotating the address by one digit position, creates structurally harmonious color palettes derived directly from the geometry of the RGB cube, never leaving RGB.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [149] [Revisiting Classic Thought Experiments to Measure Consciousness for Artificial Intelligence Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00001)
*Peter David Fagan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过守恒一致性编码（CCE）框架重新审视莱布尼茨磨坊、图灵测试和塞尔的中文屋论证，提出区分外显任务表现（W_causal,T）与内部结构复用效率（κ_T，即操作性意识）的分析框架，指出行为等价而内部组织迥异的系统在后者上存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统关于"理解"的哲学争论（如中文屋等）长期纠缠于外显行为是否足以判断智能或意识。本文试图通过引入可形式化的框架，将外显行为表现与内部组织效率分开度量，从而为经典争论提供新的分析视角，并为此后的AI安全分析奠定动机基础。

Method: 提出守恒一致性编码（CCE）框架，在一个玩具符号设定中形式化两个度量指标：任务表现（W_causal,T）衡量成功行为，操作性意识（κ_T）衡量保留内部结构支撑该行为的效率。通过对比一个无压缩查找系统与一个紧凑生成系统在该框架下的行为表现与κ_T差异来进行分析。

Result: 无压缩查找系统与紧凑生成系统可以实现可比的任务表现（W_causal,T），但在操作性意识指标κ_T上存在显著分歧：前者依赖不断膨胀的未复用映射存储，后者则复用紧凑的内部结构。

Conclusion: 该框架将"理解"的经典争议重构为外显表现与支撑该表现的内部组织之间的分离问题，并指出这种区分对后续AI安全分析具有潜在重要性。

Abstract: This research note revisits Leibniz's mill, Turing's imitation game, and Searle's Chinese Room through the Conservation-Congruent Encoding (CCE) framework. It formalises a toy symbolic setting in which successful behaviour is measured by task performance ($W_{causal,T}$), while the efficiency with which preserved internal structure supports that behaviour is measured by operational consciousness ($κ_T$). Within this setup, an uncompressed lookup system and a compact generative system can in principle achieve comparable behavioural success, yet diverge sharply in $κ_T$: the former relies on an expanding standing store of unreused mappings, whereas the latter reuses compact internal structure. The note therefore reframes classic disputes about understanding by separating outward performance from the organisation that sustains it, and motivates why this distinction may matter for later AI-safety analysis.

</details>


### [150] [AutoFOAM: The Self-Refining Autonomous OpenFOAM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00003)
*Arun Govind Neelan,A Seshaditya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoFOAM是一个基于大语言模型的自主智能体，能够仅通过自然语言指令创建、评估、运行和演化OpenFOAM仿真，通过7阶段演化循环和三种防退化机制（RAG重试上下文、字典级补丁、提示多样性改写），将生成式AI与CFD仿真结合，降低OpenFOAM使用门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源CFD求解器（如OpenFOAM）使用门槛高，需要大量专业知识和耗时的手动配置文件设置，严重阻碍了快速原型开发和CFD工作流的普及。

Method: 基于Qwen-coder 2.5-14B基础模型进行微调，使用252条文本提示覆盖7个OpenFOAM求解器、13个参数化网格模板以及y+感知数值策略。核心算法为7阶段的演化循环，并采用三种互补防退化流：RAG增强重试上下文、外科字典级修补、提示多样性改写。

Result: 提出的AutoFOAM智能体能够自主创建、评估、运行和演化OpenFOAM仿真，通过自演化机制持续改进仿真质量，并有效防止模型在反复自训练过程中的退化。

Conclusion: AutoFOAM成功将生成式AI与严谨的流体仿真相结合，加速了快速原型开发进程，推动了高级CFD工作流的民主化。

Abstract: Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) plays an important role in modern engineering, but using open-source solvers such as OpenFOAM requires considerable knowledge and skills, as well as time-consuming configuration file setup. To reduce this burden, we propose AutoFOAM - a self-evolving large language model (LLM) agent that creates, evaluates, runs, and evolves its own OpenFOAM simulations based solely on natural-language instructions. Our model is pre-trained on the Qwen-coder 2.5-14B, which is then fine-tuned on 252 text prompts targeting 7 OpenFOAM solvers, 13 parametrized mesh templates, and a y plus-aware numerical policy. The crucial element of the algorithm is a sophisticated evolution loop composed of 7 stages. To prevent model degeneration under repeated self-training, the agent employs three complementary anti-collapse streams: RAG-augmented retry context, surgical dictionary-level patching, and prompt-diversity paraphrasing. By bridging generative artificial intelligence with rigorous fluid simulations, AutoFOAM accelerates rapid prototyping and democratizes advanced CFD workflows.

</details>


### [151] [Enhancing LLMs with Context-Specific Knowledge for Mitigating Misinformation in SMEs: A RAG-based Modeling and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00006)
*Md. Samiul Islam,Iqbal H. Sarker,Chadni Islam,Ahmad Mohsin,Ahmed Ibrahim,Helge Janicke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在中小企业环境中使用向量检索增强生成（VectorRAG）和图检索增强生成（GraphRAG）两种方法来减少大型语言模型（LLM）生成内容中的幻觉和错误信息问题。在LLaMA、Mistral和Qwen等多个先进LLM上进行了实验评估，结果表明RAG增强的LLM能显著提高响应质量，减少幻觉和错误信息，从而支持更可靠、可信且上下文感知的决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中小企业越来越多地采用LLM来增强问答能力和支持商业决策，但LLM输出中的幻觉现象会产生错误信息，降低用户对系统可靠性和可信度的信心。需要有效的方法来解决这一问题。

Method: 提出了两种RAG建模方法：VectorRAG和GraphRAG，通过将外部知识源纳入建模过程来减少幻觉。实验在多个SOTA LLM（LLaMA、Mistral、Qwen）上进行，评估指标包括有用响应生成能力、幻觉风险、上下文相关性以及人类可解释性。

Result: 实验结果表明，RAG增强的LLM（无论是VectorRAG还是GraphRAG）能够显著提高响应质量，有效减少幻觉和错误信息，从而为中小企业提供更可靠、可信且上下文感知的决策支持。

Conclusion: RAG方法能够有效缓解LLM在中小企业应用中的幻觉问题，增强决策支持系统的可信度和可靠性，为RAG在中小企业场景中的实际部署提供了有力依据。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs), a part of artificial intelligence (AI), are increasingly being adopted by Small and Medium Enterprises (SMEs) to enhance question-answering capabilities and support business decision-making processes. However, hallucinations in LLM-generated outputs can serve as a source of misinformation, reducing user confidence in their reliability and trustworthiness within SMEs. Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has emerged as a promising approach to address this challenge by incorporating external knowledge sources into the modeling process. In this paper, we present VectorRAG and GraphRAG modeling approaches to mitigate hallucinations and misinformation risks and evaluate their effectiveness in SME environments. Our experimental evaluation is conducted on multiple state-of-the-art LLMs, including LLaMA, Mistral, and Qwen, to assess performance in terms of useful response generation, risk of hallucination, contextual relevance, as well as human-interpretation. The results demonstrate that RAG-enhanced LLMs can significantly improve response quality by reducing hallucinations and misinformation, thereby supporting more reliable, trustworthy, and context-aware decision-making in SME environments.

</details>


### [152] [Energy Efficiency of Locally Deployed LLMs: A Preliminary Quantitative GPU Power Benchmark on Consumer Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00008)
*Philipp M. Zähl,Anika Hennig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对九个开源LLM（1B到7B参数）在单块消费级GPU（RTX 4060Ti 16GB）上进行硬件级能源基准测试，使用Ollama推理引擎并采样GPU功耗，发现模型架构和量化策略比参数量更能决定能效，其中gemma3:1b和llama3.2:1b最优（0.56和0.65 J/token），而带内部推理的模型会产生异常高的每提示能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地部署LLM因隐私需求和本地推理偏好而日益流行，但消费级硬件上的能耗成本缺乏系统量化，现有基准主要关注准确性而忽略能源效率。

Method: 在RTX 4060Ti 16GB单GPU上，通过Ollama推理引擎运行九个1B到7B参数的开源LLM，使用nvidia-smi以2Hz频率采样GPU功耗，在固定提示集上评估平均/峰值功率、每提示总能耗(J/prompt)、每输出token能耗(J/token)及吞吐量(tok/s)。

Result: 模型架构和量化策略是能效的主要驱动因素。gemma3:1b和llama3.2:1b能耗最低（0.56和0.65 J/token）且吞吐最高（>170 tok/s）；7B-Mistral每token能耗比最优模型高4.4倍；qwen3.5:2b因扩展内部推理导致每提示能耗异常偏高。

Conclusion: 能效评估需超越参数规模，关注架构与量化策略；效率指标应区分token生成模式（如推理token与输出token），以避免能耗度量失真。

Abstract: The local deployment of large language models (LLMs) is gaining traction due to privacy concerns and the desire for on-premise inference. However, the energy costs on consumer hardware remain poorly characterized, as most benchmarks focus solely on accuracy. This paper presents a reproducible, hardware-level energy benchmark of nine open-source LLMs (1B to 7B parameters) executed on a single consumer GPU (RTX 4060Ti 16GB). Using the Ollama inference engine, GPU power draw was sampled at 2Hz via nvidia-smi across a fixed prompt set. We evaluate mean/peak power, total energy per prompt (J/prompt), energy per output token (J/token), and throughput (tok/s). Our findings suggest that factors beyond raw parameter count, including model architecture and quantization strategy, drive energy efficiency. Specifically, gemma3:1b and llama3.2:1b achieve the lowest energy cost (0.56 J/token and 0.65 J/token) and the highest throughput (>170 tok/s). In contrast, the 7B-Mistral model consumes up to 4.4x more energy per token than the most efficient model. Notably, qwen3.5:2b exhibits anomalously high per-prompt energy due to extended internal reasoning, highlighting the need to distinguish between token generation modes in efficiency metrics.

</details>


### [153] [CoT-Core: Accelerating LLM Evaluation via CoT-Aware Coreset Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00014)
*Qihua Pan,Zhenheng Tang,Peijie Dong,Xiang Liu,Huacan Wang,Bo Li,Xiaowen Chu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CoT-Core框架，通过零样本思维链推理轨迹在潜在空间中对问题按内在逻辑等价性聚类，实现无需历史日志的训练无关核心集选择，大幅降低LLM评估成本并保持高保真度评分估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评估在持续开发过程中计算开销高昂；现有核心集选择方法要么面临严重冷启动瓶颈需要大量历史日志（如项目反应理论），要么存在表面词汇偏差，忽略了任务背后的推理流形。

Method: CoT-Core：一个训练无关的核心问题选择框架。利用LLM展开零样本思维链推理轨迹，将这些路径投影到潜在空间，按问题的内在逻辑等价性（而非表面文本相似性）进行聚类，从而实现核心问题选择。

Result: 在GSM8K、MMLU、MMLU-Pro和GPQA上的实验表明，CoT-Core大幅降低评估成本的同时保持高保真度分数估计，并揭示了推理感知剪枝的边界条件——其有效性本质上受任务复杂性门控。

Conclusion: CoT-Core通过推理感知的核心集选择有效解决了LLM评估中的冷启动和文本偏差问题，但方法效果受任务复杂度约束，需要在实际应用中考虑任务特性。

Abstract: Evaluating Large Language Models (LLMs) incurs prohibitive computational overhead during continuous development processes. While coreset selection accelerates evaluation, existing methods either suffer from a severe ``cold start'' bottleneck requiring massive historical logs (e.g., Item Response Theory) or exhibit a surface lexical bias that misses the underlying reasoning manifold of tasks. We propose CoT-Core, a novel training-free core question selection framework. Recognizing that lexically disparate questions can share equivalent underlying logic, CoT-Core prompts LLMs to unroll zero-shot Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning trajectories. Projecting these paths into a latent space effectively clusters questions by intrinsic logical equivalence rather than superficial text similarity. Extensive experiments on GSM8K, MMLU, MMLU-Pro, and GPQA demonstrate that CoT-Core drastically reduces evaluation costs while maintaining high-fidelity score estimation, and delineate the boundary conditions of reasoning-aware pruning, revealing that its efficacy is intrinsically gated by task complexity.

</details>


### [154] [Optimization and Constraint Modeling using LLMs with a Retrieval Augmented Generation Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00015)
*Prateek Roy,Akash Singirikonda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于检索增强生成（RAG）的流水线，利用合成的优化问题数据集来提升大语言模型的优化建模能力。在Qwen 3 30B Instruct模型上，该方法将NL4OPT准确率从40%提升至72%，MAMO Easy从40%提升至56%，MAMO Complex从32%提升至56%，验证了合成数据集配合检索增强是微调的有效替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 优化建模和约束建模需要深厚的领域专家知识和建模语言熟练度，然而当前大语言模型在生成优化模型时经常产生结构不一致或不完整的表述，特别是在组合优化场景下。作者旨在探索是否可以无需昂贵的模型微调，通过检索增强生成技术来提升LLM的优化建模性能。

Method: 提出一个RAG流水线：1）使用Text2Zinc数据集的种子描述和LLM创建的专业人设，合成500个优化问题，以JSON格式规范并关联可验证的Python求解器脚本；2）将这些问题编码存入Chroma向量数据库；3）推理时检索语义相似的问题作为上下文指导LangChain LLM代理；4）在Qwen 3 30B Instruct模型下，使用NL4OPT、MAMO Easy和MAMO Complex三个基准测试集进行评估。

Result: 在三个基准测试集上，准确率分别从40%提升至72%（NL4OPT）、40%提升至56%（MAMO Easy）、32%提升至56%（MAMO Complex）。语义验证的合成示例大幅提升了求解准确率和模型结构完整性。

Conclusion: 合成数据集生成与检索增强的结合是微调的有效替代方案，表明领域特定合成语料库配合检索增强可以作为一种实用的部署路径，在不需要昂贵的模型重新训练的情况下，将LLM驱动的优化工具应用于实际决策支持场景。

Abstract: Both optimization modeling and constraint modeling are non-trivial problems requiring deep domain expertise and proficiency in modeling formalism languages. Despite their importance across logistics, healthcare, and supply chain management, current large language models regularly produce structurally inconsistent or incomplete optimization formulations, particularly in combinatorial settings. This paper evaluates whether a Retrieval-Augmented Generation pipeline built on a curated synthetic dataset can meaningfully improve LLM optimization modeling performance. A total of 500 optimization problems were synthesized using seed descriptions from the Text2Zinc dataset and professional personas created using an LLM, specified in JSON and associated with validated Python solver scripts. These problems were encoded in a Chroma vector database. For each inference problem, semantically similar problems were retrieved and used as contextual guidance for a LangChain LLM agent. Three benchmark testbeds were used to evaluate the proposed pipeline under the Qwen 3 30B Instruct model. Accuracy rose from 40% to 72% on NL4OPT, 40% to 56% on MAMO Easy, and 32% to 56% on MAMO Complex. The use of semantically validated synthetic examples greatly improves both solution accuracy and structure. The combination of synthetic dataset generation with retrieval augmentation provides an effective alternative to fine-tuning, suggesting that domain-specific synthetic corpora paired with retrieval augmentation can serve as a practical pathway for deploying LLM-based optimization tools in real-world decision-support contexts without costly model retraining.

</details>


### [155] [Memory Reward Inflation in Self-Improving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00017)
*Mohammad Asadolahi,Amir Amini,Samira Talebi,Amirfarhad Farhadi,Azadeh Zamanifar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究无权重更新的记忆型自改进LLM智能体中，存储评分作为代理奖励的可靠性问题。提出"回声鸿沟"（Echo Gap）失败模式：智能体偏好重用自己最自信的错误记忆，因为确认偏差使评分无法识别被高估的记忆。形式化"误差独立性假设"（EIA）作为修正奖励膨胀的必要条件，并推出无需答案的去膨胀算法LUCID，在BIRD文本转SQL基准上提升执行准确率至56.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆型自改进LLM智能体（如Memento）通过存储和检索过往经验来改进性能而不更新权重，但部署场景下缺少真实标签，存储的评分只能依赖LLM自评。这种替代会产生一个严重的失败模式——智能体倾向于重用自己最自信的错误，导致错误通过记忆累积而非抵消。作者旨在形式化这一问题并找到可修正的算法方案。

Method: 首先形式化"回声鸿沟"：当错误记忆获得膨胀的奖励时，检索机制会优先选择这些错误。作者证明检索按存储分排序时膨胀会复合，且在纯相似度检索（实际部署使用的机制）下同样发生。然后形式化误差独立性假设（EIA）——信号必须追踪真实值且其误差与记忆偏差去相关——并证明EIA是修正膨胀的必要条件，可恢复的收益是这两个量的闭式函数。基于此推出无答案依赖的去膨胀算法LUCID。

Result: LUCID算法在BIRD文本转SQL基准上达到56.9%的执行准确率，优于Memento风格自评分智能体的54.0%（平均+2.9点）和无记忆同架构智能体的52.4%。在多个记忆型自改进智能体和模型家族上均观察到回声鸿沟现象。

Conclusion: 回声鸿沟是记忆型自改进LLM智能体的系统性失败模式，需要在评分设计上同时保证信号的真实性和误差独立性。EIA是修正奖励膨胀的必要条件，而LUCID提供了首个无需真实答案即可修正该偏差的有效算法。

Abstract: Self-improving LLM agents increasingly learn from experience without updating any weights. Each episode is stored in an external memory, scored, and retrieved for similar future tasks to shape later behavior. Viewed through a reward lens, the stored score is a proxy reward for an implicit, non-parametric policy. Each retrieved episode then becomes a policy-improvement step whose reliability hinges on how that score is produced. In deployment, ground-truth labels are unavailable, so the stored reward is at best an LLM assessment. This substitution creates a failure mode, the *Echo Gap*, across the memory-based self-improving agents and model families studied. Incorrect episodes receive inflated rewards; thus, the agent preferentially reuses the very mistakes it has most confident in. Because the error compounds through memory rather than averaging out and the confirming judge's errors remain correlated with the original self-grading bias, so it cannot identify which memories are overvalued. The missing property is formalized as the *Error-Independence Assumption* (EIA), which we prove is a *necessary* condition for correcting the inflation, not merely a description of a good verifier: a usable signal must track truth *and* decorrelate its error from the memory bias, and the recoverable payoff is a closed-form function of exactly those two quantities. We further show the inflation compounds not only when retrieval ranks by the stored score but also under plain similarity retrieval which is the regime the deployed agent uses. Finally, the answer-free de-inflation algorithm LUCID delivers a consistent end-to-end gain on the BIRD text-to-SQL benchmark. It raises execution accuracy to $56.9\%$, above both a Memento-style self-graded agent ($54.0\%$, a $+2.9$-point mean gain across seeds) and a memory-less agent of identical architecture ($52.4\%$).

</details>


### [156] [RL-Lock: Reinforcement Learning for Generating Interlocking Assemblies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.01744)
*Xuyang Ma,Chaewoon Kim,Haonan Zhang,Rulin Chen,Ziqi Wang,Peng Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出RL-Lock，首个基于强化学习的互锁装配体生成框架。它将互锁装配体的生成视为序列决策问题，通过结构化动作分块与MCTS引导的策略-价值学习，无需手工设计的搜索启发式，即可在大型组合搜索空间中高效生成互锁装配体，尤其在现有方法耗时过长或无法找到有效解的困难场景中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互锁装配体因其结构稳定性在现实应用中被广泛使用，但生成互锁装配体本质上是一个形状分解问题，需要在体素网格中将目标3D物体分割为指定数量的互锁部件。现有方法依赖手工设计的搜索启发式，在困难情形下往往耗时过长甚至无法找到有效解。

Method: 本文将互锁装配体生成问题建模为序列决策问题，其中智能体反复决定每个体素应分配给哪个部件。提出RL-Lock框架，结合结构化动作分块（structured action chunking）与MCTS引导的策略-价值学习（policy-value learning），以高效导航大型组合搜索空间。

Result: 实验表明RL-Lock能够有效生成互锁装配体，尤其在现有方法耗时过长或无法找到有效解的挑战性场景中表现出色。

Conclusion: RL-Lock作为首个无需手工搜索启发式的强化学习框架，验证了将互锁装配体生成视为序列决策问题的可行性，为复杂形状分解问题提供了新的解决范式。

Abstract: An interlocking assembly is an assembly in which component parts are connected purely through their geometric arrangement, without relying on external connectors such as glue and nails. Such assemblies have been widely used in a variety of real-world applications due to their structural stability. The problem of generating interlocking assemblies is generally formulated as a shape decomposition problem, where a target 3D object represented as a voxel grid is partitioned into a prescribed number of interlocking pieces. We observe that generating interlocking assemblies is inherently a sequential decision-making problem, where an agent repeatedly decides which piece each voxel should be assigned to. Inspired by the observation, we propose the first reinforcement learning framework RL-Lock for generating interlocking assemblies, without relying on handcrafted search heuristics as existing works did. RL-Lock combines structured action chunking with MCTS-guided policy-value learning to efficiently navigate the large combinatorial search space for interlocking assembly generation. We demonstrate through experiments that RL-Lock allows effective generation of interlocking assemblies, especially for challenging cases in which existing approaches take too long or even fail to find a valid solution.

</details>


### [157] [Request-Level Energy Attribution for Batched LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00026)
*Qi Luo,Kunlin Li,Ziwen Wang,Dongsheng Wang,Yun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出JouleShare框架，通过离线测量真实Shapley能量分配作为基准，并用轻量校准模型JCalib在在线服务时预测请求级能耗分配。实验表明token比例分配与精确Shapley值平均偏差达0.44-0.458，而JCalib可将误差降至0.116-0.177，证明token分配不是可靠的能耗代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 批处理的LLM服务虽然提高吞吐量，但GPU功耗遥测是聚合的，无法直接获取请求级能耗，导致可持续性报告、费用回充和工作负载分析缺乏请求级能耗基准。现有基准只提供模型级、相位级或token级能耗，缺乏实测的请求级真实分配值。

Method: JouleShare包含两个组件：1) 离线测试平台，通过vLLM回放请求子集，集成GPU功耗遥测并计算每个请求的精确Shapley能量（作为ground truth）；2) 轻量校准模型JCalib，从低成本请求特征学习预测Shapley份额，用于在线服务时。同时使用采样Shapley扩展到更大组规模。

Result: 在16个模型/工作负载运行中，token比例分配与精确Shapley值的归一化L1误差在静态批处理下为0.440，连续批处理下为0.458，且跨三种数据中心GPU均复现。JCalib将误差降至静态批处理0.116和连续批处理0.177，优于在线不可用的独立测量基线，同时保持批处理能量效率。

Conclusion: Token比例分配不能可靠地代表批处理执行下的边际能量消耗。通过实测Shapley ground truth校准低成本请求特征，可以实现更公平的能耗归属，为实际LLM服务中的能耗核算提供了可部署的解决方案。

Abstract: Batched LLM serving improves throughput but complicates energy accounting. GPU power telemetry is aggregate, whereas sustainability reporting, chargeback, and workload analysis often require request-level energy charges. Existing inference-energy benchmarks report model-, phase-, or token-level energy, and recent carbon-accounting work motivates Shapley fairness conceptually. Neither provides measured request-level ground truth, so how far the accounting rules used in practice deviate from a fair allocation has remained unknown. We present JouleShare, an attribution framework with two components. An offline harness establishes this ground truth by replaying request subsets under vLLM with a reproducible protocol, integrating GPU power telemetry, and computing exact Shapley energy for each request. A lightweight calibration model, JCalib, then learns to predict Shapley shares from cheap request features for use at serving time. Across 16 model/workload runs, token-proportional attribution differs from exact Shapley by 0.440 normalized L1 on average under static batching and by 0.458 under continuous batching, a gap that reproduces across three data-center GPUs. JCalib reduces this error to 0.116 under static batching and 0.177 under continuous batching, below even a standalone-measurement baseline that is unavailable online, while preserving exact batch-energy efficiency. Sampled Shapley extends the measured reference to larger group sizes, where the gap persists and a single offline calibration remains the most accurate deployable rule. The results show that token attribution is not a reliable proxy for marginal energy under batched execution, and that measured Shapley ground truth can calibrate low-cost request features toward fairer attribution.

</details>


### [158] [Motif-Mamba: network motif improved mamba for long-range sequence modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00027)
*Chonghe Hao,Yue Sun,Jian Zhang,Yansong Wang,Wangzi Yao,Yunjie Yao,Tielin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Motif-Mamba是一种结构化状态空间模型，通过在Mamba中引入基于三节点网络基序的低秩循环通路，增强隐藏状态维度间的交互通信，同时保持线性时间的循环结构，在长序列外推、语言建模和脑机接口解码任务上均优于Mamba基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mamba虽然通过选择性状态空间循环实现了线性时间的长序列建模，但其状态转移矩阵以对角度为主，限制了不同状态维度之间的显式交互，从而可能损失跨维度的信息流通能力。

Method: 提出Motif-Mamba，受三节点网络基序动力学启发，在Mamba框架中增加了一个受基序约束的低秩循环通路。该通路先将隐藏状态投影到紧凑的动力学子空间，在子空间中施加基序引导的交互，再将动力学结果映射回原始状态空间，以增强跨维通信同时维持线性时间递归结构。

Result: 在长序列外推、语言建模基准测试和脑机接口解码等多个实验中都表现出比Mamba骨干网络一致的性能提升，验证了基序引导的低秩动力学作为长序列建模的结构先验是有效的。

Conclusion: 基序引导的低秩循环通路为长序列建模提供了有效的结构先验，能够在保持Mamba高效性的同时增强其表达能力。

Abstract: Efficient long-sequence modeling remains a central challenge for large language models, as self-attention scales quadratically with sequence length. Mamba offers a linear-time alternative through selective state space recurrence, but its predominantly diagonal state transitions restrict explicit interactions among state dimensions. We propose Motif-Mamba, a structured state space model that augments Mamba with a motif-constrained low-rank recurrent pathway. Inspired by the dynamics of three-node network motifs, the proposed pathway projects hidden states into a compact dynamical subspace, imposes motif-guided interactions, and maps the resulting dynamics back to the original state space. This design enhances cross-dimensional communication while preserving the linear-time recurrent structure of Mamba. Experiments on long-sequence extrapolation, language modeling benchmarks, and brain--computer interface decoding show consistent improvements over Mamba backbones, suggesting that motif-guided low-rank dynamics provide an effective structural prior for long-range sequence modeling.

</details>


### [159] [Nova: An End-to-End MLIR Compiler for Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00029)
*Adwaid Suresh,Aparna A,Harshini V M,Jona Delcy C A,Killi Uma Maheswara Rao,Ram Charan Golla,Surendra Vendra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Nova是一个自动化端到端JIT编译器，通过跨操作边界融合、内存层次优化和寄存器级调优，实现对硬件映射的绝对控制，在RTX 3060上GPU吞吐量较PyTorch提升最多10.6%，较XLA提升4.4%，并将内存占用降低最多29%，使PyTorch无法训练的1.44亿参数模型在12GB消费级GPU上成功训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型规模化训练高度依赖高层数学操作到物理硬件的有效映射。现有的高层张量框架（如PyTorch）采用即时执行模式，缺乏全图可见性以及对硬件和内存的细粒度控制，无法原生最大化物理硬件利用率，这构成了性能瓶颈。

Method: Nova设计为自动化的端到端JIT编译器，核心方法包括：(1) 捕获即时执行并将前向和反向传播统一为单一值语义方言，解锁激进的全图优化；(2) 利用解析配置器（Analytic Configurator）基于算术强度确定性地推导最优执行调度，将搜索时间降至零；(3) 依托结构化哈希运行时（structural hashing runtime）直接从计算结构合成细粒度内核。

Result: 在RTX 3060上，Nova在TF32矩阵乘法的多数形状上与cuBLAS和XLA匹配或适度超越，相对误差严格控制在5e-4以内。模型层面，在4200万参数模型上，Nova比PyTorch吞吐量提升最多10.6%，比XLA提升4.4%。内存方面，相较PyTorch最多降低29%的内存占用，使得1.44亿参数模型在12GB消费级GPU上以17,900 tokens/s成功训练，而PyTorch在此场景下发生OOM失败。

Conclusion: Nova通过端到端JIT编译、解析式调度推导和结构驱动的内核合成，在不牺牲数值精度的情况下，实现了超越现有框架的吞吐性能和显著的内存效率提升，证明了自动化编译技术在突破深度学习硬件利用率瓶颈方面的可行性。

Abstract: The performance of deep learning models at scale relies heavily on how effectively high-level mathematical operations are mapped to underlying physical hardware. While high-level tensor frameworks provide flexible abstractions for model design, their eager execution models inherently lack the whole-graph visibility and granular control over hardware and memory required to maximize physical hardware utilization natively.
